## Mechanism verdict

Audit verdict: **POLICY_SEPARABLE**

Bounded conclusion: **C2 AND C3 ARE POLICY-SEPARABLE IN DETERMINISTIC DEVELOPMENT**

At least one valid reachable development state causes C2 and C3
to choose different experiments under the frozen replay-cost
catalogue.

Across the corresponding active-state graph, equal-cost C3 matches
C2, so the observed divergence is attributable to the calibrated
cost asymmetry under this deterministic mechanism audit.

This establishes policy separability only.

---

## Equal-cost negative control

The equal-cost C3 ranking matched C2 across every active reachable state.

No mismatching state was silently discarded.

---

## Open-set and confirmation boundary

Singleton states were recorded separately from ordinary ranking states.

Their corresponding confirming probe retains its ordinary calibrated
replay cost.

No free-confirmation rule was introduced.

The existing out-of-RFS and open-set implementations were not modified.

This audit does not establish:

- lower total diagnosis cost;
- H1 support;
- repair correctness;
- production efficiency;
- model-backed robustness;
- real-world debugging-cost reduction;
- or protected P09.4 validity.

No C0-C3 performance comparison was executed.

No final P09.4 scientific budget was selected.

No protected P09.4 work was performed.
